# Skills · R · part 18

846 entries, report-publishing to requirements-specification. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **report-publishing**: The Zebra-specific things to do around publishing a report to a Power BI or Fabric workspace. Microsoft's powerbi-report-management skill does the upload; this covers the visual-track decision that has to be made before you publish, the Zebra licence in the service, and the post-publish refresh without which a report built from a portable .pbip is silently empty. Use when the user says "publish", "upload", "push to Fabric", "deploy the report", "share it with the team", or asks why a published report shows no data. For authoring, use report-authoring. ([zebrabi/zebra-bi-agentic](https://github.com/zebrabi/zebra-bi-agentic/tree/HEAD/plugins/zebra-bi/skills/report-publishing) · ★2)
